- **Debris identities** (Sevaan: "if we know what the object is, it should be in the fact"): scripts/build-debris.py → data/debris.json { id: { what, art?, fact } } for all 453 debris cards, from catalogue names plus satellitemap.space / isstracker.pl alternative names (looked up 2026-10-15). lore.js loads it (`debrisOf`); factFor uses its fact after hand lore; the card subtitle reads "SYLDA · 1984" etc.; art-keys uses `deb-<art>` only once that picture exists (ART_FILES). 295 are plain fragments ("A fragment of Cosmos 2251, launched in 1993…"). Debris pictures and the engraved restyle of all art are in progress (scratchpad restyle-all/), not imported yet.

2026-10-15 · v0.1.707: Equator-S (25068) has its own picture, equator-s, drawn from Sevaan's photo (Desktop image-1000-61a8…jpeg) in the new engraved style with the card colours. It used to share classic-satellite-1.

2026-10-15 · v0.1.708: review.html shows restyled pictures as they arrive (design/restyle-preview/<art>.webp + manifest.json, refreshed every minute) at full opacity; cards still on original art are at 50%. scripts/sync-restyle-preview.sh copies and lists them; rejected restyles are left out.

2026-10-15 · v0.1.709: card pictures are lowered per picture, not all by 11% (Sevaan: "use some judgement"). scripts/art-frame.py measures each picture's subject in the 4:3 window and lowers it by 15% minus its top, at most 12%, and never so far that the subject's bottom leaves the window (very wide rows count as Earth, not subject) → js/art-frame.js ART_SHIFT; card.js sets the img top. 72 of 165 move, the rest stay put. Rerun after importing art. Also rejected the deb-avum-adaptor restyle (it swapped the object and took the style sample's colours).

2026-10-15 · v0.1.710: framing keeps a 6% margin under the subject (was 1.5%; Ampte/CCE's panel was cut off): 51 of 165 lowered. Restyled preview art gets its own framing (design/restyle-preview/frame.js, written by sync-restyle-preview.sh) on review.html.

2026-10-15 · v0.1.711: the per-picture shift, zoom and top fade are all removed (Sevaan: "the fade is a bad idea… regen with the different aspect ratio"). Pictures show as drawn; framing gets fixed by regenerating the art at 4:3 with the object centred and room above it.

2026-10-15 · v0.1.712: unidentified ISS-released objects ("ISS OBJECT YM"…) get the smallsat picture, a subtitle "Unidentified CubeSat · from the ISS · 2026" (identified ones: "<role> · from the ISS · year") and a fact explaining the letters. Name check: CelesTrak SATCAT (our source, refreshed daily) has 69795 = PROVES-ELECTRA (1998-067YL) and 69796 = ISS OBJECT YM; isstracker.pl still shows 69796 as "YL", an older name. Official records give every ISS-released object the station's 1998-11-20 launch; our date is the estimated cargo flight (scripts/iss-deployed.mjs).

2026-10-15 · v0.1.713: new pictures from Sevaan's references: essa (all 9 ESSA cards: tall 18-sided drum with the side camera), lusat (LUSAT LO-19 cube with antennas), story-starman (the real cherry-red first-gen Tesla Roadster; fact and a CAR stat now name it), story-ringside (Saturn with correct flat rings). Restyle pipeline is now two steps: restyle only (run.py), then frame.py centres each object inside the area below the labels (x 5-95%, y 19-95%) by shrinking/moving the finished picture and having the model paint only the empty margin; review.html shows the framed results.

2026-10-15 · v0.1.718 (Sevaan):
- **Tap to wake the sky** (design/tap-to-resume.html A): back in the app with motion not live after 1.5 s (`checkMotionBack` on visibilitychange), the target circle turns orange and pulses with a fingertip tapping in it, "TAP TO WAKE THE SKY / Motion pauses while you're away" under it (#wake, `syncWake` each tick; hidden during the tour, panels and onboarding). Replaces the "Tap anywhere…" standing message.
- **Reminder buttons are toggles** (Tonight page #ob-remind, bad-weather #nt-remind): on = dark glass, gold outline, gold bell, "REMINDER ON · 9:00" and "TURN OFF"; a success haptic when set, a tick when cancelled (`remindDone` / `remindUndo`). The Tonight page's reminder now uses the shared pass key, so its Tonight-tab bell agrees.

2026-10-15 · v0.1.719 (Sevaan got a headliner alert on a cloudy night):
- **Notifications only on a good night:** weather.js `goodSkyAt(wx, t)`: the forecast must be known and every forecast hour within 90 min of t clear (plus current conditions when t is within the hour). Unknown weather no longer counts as clear for Look up or Catch of the day (skyClearAt is unchanged elsewhere). The phone's alert ("Tonight's headliner in 10 min… Look up!") came from an older build without the weather check.
- **Catch of the day fires 5 minutes before** (was 10): "Catch of the day in 5 min". Reminders you set are still 10 minutes before.
- **Names keep their acronyms:** facts.js titleCase has a much longer ACRONYMS list (ESSA, TOS, TIROS, GGSE, OPS, DOVE, MOST, IMAGE…) and keeps 2–3 letter codes before a number in capitals ("AO-7", "SO-50"). "Essa 4 (Tos-B)" → "ESSA 4 (TOS-B)".
- **Star name fixes** (constellations.js STAR_FIX, from the fact research): ten stars carried a neighbour's or an unofficial name (η Aql "Tarazed", κ¹ Pup "Markab"…) and now show their Bayer name; κ Sco is Girtab; Regor and Alsephina are Vela, ν Pup is Puppis (with matching codes).
- **Natural facts tightened:** Moon, Mars, Saturn, Arcturus, Deneb, Polaris, Sirius.
- **Fact research in progress** (pre-launch writing review): lore.js reads data/facts.json (`sats` by NORAD id, `rockets` = varied lines per rocket family) and constellations.js reads data/star-facts.json (by HIP); both fall back cleanly when the files are absent. Not shipped yet.

2026-10-15 · v0.1.720: **researched card facts shipped** (pre-launch writing review, Sevaan: "Ship it"). data/facts.json: `sats` = 1,249 satellites with their own fact + source (Gunter's Space Page, NSSDCA, Wikipedia, eoPortal…), `rockets` = 183 regex entries / 350 lines across all 175 rocket families (picked by NORAD id; 309 distinct lines over 1,568 stages). data/star-facts.json: all 729 constellation stars (by HIP). Order in factFor: hand lore → debris → researched/rocket → ISS object → series → family → purpose → computed. Corrected wrong old facts (OSCAR 23–32 = Transit navigation, Jason-1, CYGNSS, Poppy "GGSE"/OPS, Zaliv Kosmos…). Review board: https://claude.ai/artifact/MAH7xMZKeMxd6hGxEA3V8e. Research scratch and merge script (style checks) in the session scratchpad facts/.

2026-10-16 · v0.1.722: **framing editor on review.html** (Sevaan's idea). In the card viewer, tap the picture: drag to move, drag a corner handle / pinch / scroll to zoom, dashed box = where the object should sit (x 5–95%, y 19–95%), then Save framing. Saved per picture in localStorage 'reviewFrames' as { x, y, z, py } (shift in picture-widths, zoom around the window centre at py down the picture) and shown on every tile using it (teal outline). "Copy framing (N)" copies the JSON; Claude bakes it into the art with scratchpad restyle-all/bake.py (crop, or the model paints any uncovered margin, colour-matched and feathered; originals kept in framed-before-bake/), then scripts/import-art.py. No CSS shifting in the game itself.

- v0.1.738 (Sevaan: janky transitions):
  - **Collection jank:** every empty tile slot (all cards not yet scrolled near, ~4,850) ran the loading shimmer forever (`.tile-slot:empty` in css/ui.css), which kept the card viewer's flip janky. Empty slots are now a plain placeholder; only a picture still loading shimmers. Measured at 4× CPU: 4,924 → 75 running animations, flip p95 117 → 17 ms.
  - **View in collection:** after a catch, a loaded Collection re-reads the sighting log in place (main.js collectionStale posts {sc:'refresh'}; cards-page `ingest`/`refreshSightings`) instead of reloading the whole page, so it's ready to slide straight up.
  - rocket-stage-1 framing baked (frames-2.json).
- v0.1.739: Collection 'Up tonight' slots (Sevaan): tap previews the card in the viewer (the Up tonight cards become the viewer's list, restored on close; the viewer's next-pass pill has the bell) instead of setting a reminder blind; the pill reads 'UP TONIGHT / 7:05 PM · E' and wraps instead of cutting off; every tile's set header is one fixed-height line so the dividers line up.
- v0.1.740 **animation / jank sweep** (Sevaan asked; harness in session scratchpad pw/perf/: run.mjs measures frame times and long tasks per interaction in Chromium at 4× CPU, stress-loops switcher/Settings/panel ×10–12, checks heap/node growth; prof.mjs CPU-profiles the sky; idle.mjs lists running animations):
  - Sky: the Milky Way and cloud WebGL layers render at half resolution (scale 0.5; copying the GL canvas into the 2D sky was ~60% of a frame's work). Drag-pan p50 42 → 33 ms, janky frames 64 → 21; idle p50 32 → 17 ms.
  - Nothing animates at idle any more: the hidden Collection pauses all CSS animations (html.away via {sc:'away'}; removed on open/show), and the welcome card's filled animations are cancelled once the sky starts.
  - Stress: no leaks (heap 64 → 64 MB, +38 nodes after 32 toggles). Collection, viewer flips/swipes, panels: p95 ≈ 10 ms.
  - Still heavy (one-offs): first sky frame when the tour starts (~270 ms long task at 4×), the Tonight page while the planner lands (16 long tasks), Collection slide-down as the sky wakes (one ~150 ms frame).
- v0.1.741 perf sweep, round 2 (Sevaan: "fix those"):
  - `dayGhosts` (daytime pass markers) is memoized per tonight plan / collection size / half-minute; it re-sorted tonight's passes every frame and was a quarter of the sky's work by day.
  - Layout is measured every 2 s (and on resize) instead of right after renderTarget's DOM writes 4× a second (each read forced a full layout); #target's rect is skipped when hidden.
  - Satellite model's first sweep (2.6 s) and the cloud shader (sky.warmClouds, 3.2 s) are warmed while the welcome card is up, like the Milky Way.
  - The hidden Collection preloads only once the tour and Tonight page are closed (requestIdleCallback).
  - The sky's first frame after a pause only draws; refreshAbove and the once-a-second chip/weather/banner work run 200–400 ms later (skyPaused).
  - Results at 4× CPU: tour start longest task 520 → 224 ms; Tonight page long tasks 45 → 7; drag-pan p50 42 → 25 ms, janky 64 → 7; sky idle p50 32 → 16 ms; Collection slide-down worst frame 141 → 126 ms (now one raster frame, no long task); stress loops ≤ 1 janky frame.

Messages up-remind / up-show to Explore, which finds the exact pass in state.tonight (by id + start), runs remindPass / closes the Collection and runs showMePass, and replies find-reminded. The card shrinks while the panel shows so the buttons fit; the small next-pass pill hides then. Website: the button says reminders work in the app.

- 2026-10-16 · TestFlight build 1.0 (10) archived (web v0.1.752: Plus card sheet, Rare alerts, Plus Member patch); opened in Organizer, not uploaded.
- v0.1.754 bug hunt (playtest harness on two phones, Collection tabs, Plus sheet, plus a code review of the last 3 days):
  - Tonight's run showed a "Last chance" pill on every headliner (lcTag also returns ' · headliner'); the pill now checks fallAt.
  - Catch of the day respects quiet hours (11 pm–7 am), like Rare alerts (Night owl headliners can be after midnight).
  - scheduleNotes runs one at a time (notesQ): overlapping runs could let a stale clear-forecast run schedule an alert after a cloudy one cancelled it.
  - Orbit view: a tap timer firing after a swipe/close no longer builds a hidden globe on the old card.
  - Collection: while the viewer shows the Up tonight list (openUpNext), render() waits (upHold) and redraws on close, so a 'tonight'/'refresh' message can't swap the list under the viewer.
  - Red night mode: the fade ring track and the Earth's-shadow edge/label are red, not gold; the "NOW!" pulse is still under reduced motion.
  - Unfound story cards: tile aria-label says "Secret story", and search can't find them.
  - roleOf: the catch-all "…SAT → Small satellite" only applies after the UCS purpose data (RADARSAT etc. got the right role when UCS knows it).

  - "Best day" counts by calendar date (a morning Sun and evening Earth on the 16th were split across two noon-to-noon nights) and says "1 card" in the singular.

Entitlement com.apple.developer.ubiquity-kvstore-identifier. Reset everything clears it too.

The daylight fossil uses the same 4 s hold.

Label is now two lines, "EARTH'S SHADOW" / "Satellites past this line go dark", placed above (else below) the nearest edge point whose label box is on screen and clear of the sight and the ticket column.

Same cylinder model as orbit.js isSunlit, so the line matches exactly when the target fades.

- 2026-10-17 · The Moon first (Sevaan: the Wow! signal sat 3° from the Moon and the circle flickered between them). In secretsState's hold(): while the Moon is still new to you, the Wow! signal is never shown or found, and any hold-secret within 10° of the Moon is skipped, so it can't take the circle from the Moon.
- 2026-10-17 · Earth's shadow outline is now drawn from geometry, horizon to horizon (Sevaan: the traced line stopped partway down and the shade went jagged where the edge runs steeply). shadowShape: the edge is the circle where the cylinder of radius R behind Earth meets the shell at R + H, sampled every 0.75° and seen from the observer; runs break only below the horizon. Shade strips are slanted quads whose ends follow the bisected edge at both azimuths (plain strips only where the edge turns); `shadeQuad` replaced shadeCell.

It ends when you catch it (obFinish clears tourCatchId) or tap it away (pinnedId changes); held toasts then show as usual.

Voyager 1 and 2 are now the spacecraft (dish, bus, magnetometer boom, RTG arm), the Pioneers a face-on dish with RTG arms and boom. Checked all 12 kinds side by side (santa, roadster, voyager, voyager2, pioneer10, pioneer11, newhorizons, wow, arecibo, halley, sputnik, laika).
- 2026-10-17 · During the tour cards the circle sits above them (Sevaan: step 2 talks about the circle, which was hidden behind the card). tourCenterY() in js/main.js: while body.ob-tour, the sky's centre is halfway between the header's bottom and #ob-deck's top (+6), never lower than the usual middle; it eases there and back (10% a frame; instant with Reduce Motion), so when the cards go the circle glides down to the middle of the phone.
- 2026-10-17 · After the tour the circle drops back to the middle 3× faster (30% a frame, about 0.15 s; rising during the tour stays 10%).
- 2026-10-17 · Perf pass (Sevaan: opening the Collection was janky for a moment):
  - The Collection's tile IntersectionObserver now uses `root: document` (the frame's own viewport). With the default root (the top window) the off-screen Collection built no tiles until it slid up, so the first screenful (full mini cards, art, cascade) was built mid-slide. Now they're built and their pictures loaded in the background (checked: 14 tiles, 12/12 images ready before opening). Tile images switch to loading="eager" when the tile is built.
  - On show, the Collection restarts its paused animations 460 ms later, after the slide-up lands (`awayT`), not at the start.
  - The target's full card art is fetched and decoded while you line it up (`warmArt`, last 12 kept), so the reveal has its picture ready.
- 2026-10-17 · Notification copy rewritten (Sevaan's writing agent; never names the app, the icon already does; time or "Now" first in every title):
  - Clear night: "{Tomorrow|weekday}: clear skies ahead" / "The forecast looks good. See what's passing over and plan a few catches."
  - Daily look up: "Until {until}: best viewing" / "{N} cards you haven't collected are up now. Go find your next." (none new: "It's dark and clear. See what's in your sky.")
  - Catch of the day: "{time}: catch of the day" / "Catch {Name} for +25 XP. Look high in the {south}."
  - Rare alert: "{time}: a rare find" / "Look {west} for {Name}. You haven't collected this one yet." (fleets: "{time}: a new {fleet} batch" / "… One more for your {fleet} card.")
  - Pass reminder: "{time}: {Name}" / "Look {west}. At its peak: high in the {south}."
  - Until-dark bell, each with its own title: best pass "{time}: tonight's best pass" / "Look {south} for {Name}, {40}° above the horizon."; dark "Now: time to look up" / "It's dark enough to start collecting. See what's in your sky."; busiest "Now: rush hour overhead" / "{N} satellites, all up at once. How many can you catch?"
  - Streak: "This week: keep your streak" / "One sighting is all it takes. See what's up tonight."

- 2026-10-17 · The SPACE COLLECTOR wordmark is plain text now, not a link (Sevaan: tapping it reloaded the app). index.html and cards.html use `<span class="wordmark">`; .wordmark is display:block, default cursor, not selectable.
- 2026-10-17 · Explore's header stays on top of the Collection (Sevaan: the Collection slid up with an identical second header). #collection-frame is clipped (clip-path inset(safe-top + 80px …)) so only the part under the header's dividing line slides up; the Collection hides its own header while embedded (keeps its space); Explore's header turns solid (and covers the status-bar strip) while body.col-open, its testing stopwatch hidden. A card or stats opened in the Collection (body.col-modal) unclips the frame to take the whole screen. Explore's Settings button over the Collection runs settingsOverCollection() (the old Collection Settings flow).
- 2026-10-17 · The Collection iframe loads `cards.html?embed=1&v=${VERSION}` (COL_SRC) so phones don't keep a cached cards.html with old stylesheets after an update (Sevaan saw its old header under Explore's). Embedded, the Collection has `overscroll-behavior-y: none` so pulling down at the top doesn't drag the page under the header.
- 2026-10-17 · The Collection's pull-down stretch is back (Sevaan likes the elasticity); with its own header hidden, the stretch only shows dark background under Explore's header.
- 2026-10-17 · Moon phase stamps (Sevaan, canvas "Playable Over Days" A): the Moon's eight phases are stamps on the Moon card, like a fleet's launches. card-model.js: MOON_PHASES, moonPhaseAt(t) (mean synodic month from the 2000-01-06 new Moon; checked against Oct 2026: new 10th, first quarter 18th, full 26th), moonPhaseOf(key), stampKeyFor(o, t) gives Moon sightings `MOON:<0-7>`, and sightingKeys reads older Moon sightings' phase from their time. main.js passes the time (isNewFind: a phase you haven't stamped is a new find, so the circle turns orange and you Collect it; the sighting stores its stampKey). Reveal: "NEW PHASE STAMP", toast "NEW STAMP · {PHASE} · n OF 8 PHASES", all 8 → "All 8 phases of the Moon". Card front (card.js moonBlock): "PHASES STAMPED · n / 8", newest phase named, eight moon icons (phasePath) lit when stamped, newest ringed, then the fact. Card level stays nights-based.
- 2026-10-17 · Cloudy weeks don't break the observing streak (Sevaan). main.js recordSkyNights() saves each forecast night (localStorage skyNights {dayNum: 1 cloudy | 0 clear}, 120 days) when the weather or tonight's plan arrives; progress.js streaks() skips a week with no sighting and 4+ cloudy nights (neither adds nor breaks). Unit-tested: weeks 0,1,3,4 seen + cloudy week 2 → streak 4 (2 without the cloud record). Bounties and the cloudy-night page were dropped (too close to missions and the weather page).
- 2026-10-17 · FIX (regression from the flip sweep): the catch reveal showed the card front mirrored mid-turn in WebKit, because Safari ignores backface-visibility on #reveal-card (its children have their own transforms). reveal.js swaps faces again, now on the edge-on frame read from #rv-flipper's animation clock (rAF loop on currentTime ≥ flipMs/2), not a timer. Verified with the playtest's WebKit frame trace (scratchpad pw/better/catch2.mjs): back at 1.0/1.2/1.4 s, front the right way round at 1.6 s.
- 2026-10-17 · FIX (regression from the tour-circle lift): tourCenterY read #sky-header and #ob-deck rects every tick during the tour, forcing a full layout per frame (jank playtest: 378–400 ms frames at 6× CPU, 243 ms of it forced layout). Now measured at most every 500 ms (tourMeasuredAt/tourTop/tourDeck).
- 2026-10-17 · FIX (bug hunt, regressions from today): (1) the Wow!/near-Moon secret gate used isNewFind(Moon), which is true for any unstamped phase, so it came back every phase; it now checks owning the Moon card at all (ownedKeys.has('moon')). (2) The card viewer's next-pass panel could stay on "Finding its next pass…" forever: the find-passes reply landed during openViewer's art wait (viewer not open yet) and was dropped; the handler now also renders while `opening`. Verified with the playtest's bugs/f8.mjs (panel filled within 5 s).

- 2026-10-17 · Playtest batch 3 (sound and haptics):
  - sfx.js: the two-at-once cap frees a slot when the file actually goes quiet (audibleLength: last sample over 2% of peak, measured on load), not at its silent tail; 'arrive' is never capped. The reveal whoosh and XP ticks no longer drop.
  - Lock haptic only for something collectable, once per target per 1.5 s; lock hysteresis (once locked it holds to 1.2× the ring). Plane haptic shares the sound's 4 s gap.
  - buzz() takes a kind by name ('warning', 'success'…); native.js `feel(kind)` = haptic with the Vibration pref, for the Collection. Reminders are felt once, in setReminder, after the result (success / warning); the early or duplicate ones are gone. Warning + shake() on failed reminders, purchase errors; warning on save failure and location denied.
  - Reveal: chips sound like what they are (rank-up → rankup, mission → mission + light haptic, patch → patch); on a rank-up the bar fills the old rank, flashes gold (#rv-rank.wrap) with rankup + success, then fills the new one (progressGain rank.prev). Rank chip pops in. A new Moon phase / launch stamp gets a medium payoff haptic instead of the repeat tick.
  - Collection haptics: open a card, swipe between cards, double-tap flip (attachFlip, everywhere), flick closed, tab changes, open a patch.
  - Secret hold ticks once a second; the fossil find is a success haptic like the other secrets.

- 2026-10-18 · v0.1.859: secrets in the circle are drawn about the size of a locked-on satellite or rocket stage (~26 px across), not 4.2× (Sevaan: Voyager filled the circle). sky.js SECRET_PX holds each drawing's measured width at scale 1 (scratchpad playtest/app/sec.mjs measures them); in-circle scale = 26 / width, never below the 1.2 used elsewhere. Remeasure if a secret's drawing changes.
- 2026-10-18 · v0.1.860: after you collect (or view) the thing you were steered to (Aim at it, Show me, a tap on the sky), main.js `letGoOf(obj)` releases the pin (pinnedId, targetId, guidePass, sticky), so the circle goes back to naming whatever is in it. Before, the pin stayed and its View card followed the circle around the sky (Sevaan's "Saturn view card stuck" report). Called from capture (new catch and collected-this-pass) and viewOwned.
- 2026-10-18 · v0.1.861 (Sevaan: the sky slider was hard to move): #skybar handles every touch itself (main.js grab: pointerdown anywhere on the bar jumps the knob to the finger and follows it); #sky-range is pointer-events: none (draws it, keeps keyboard use), since on iPhone a touch on the native track did nothing unless it landed on the knob. The bar's touch band reaches 14px further up into the sky (padding-top), clear of the switcher below.
- 2026-10-18 · v0.1.862 (Sevaan: the update notice showed full width for a moment before becoming the chip): standing messages (update ready, time travel, check heading; main.js showBanner) now go straight into the chip under the header on the right (#toast-tray, icon + kind line ›), never the full-width banner; time travel's changing clock updates in place; tap runs the action. Replaces v0.1.858's 6 s tuck for them.
- 2026-10-18 · v0.1.863 · Sound picks shipped (Sevaan, from the sound board): the original designed set ("old") for collect, arrive, every rarity stinger, shiny, mission, patch, coinroll, complete, fossil, ufo, welcome, step, toast, toggle, page, swipe; UI SFX cues for lock (zen snap), flip (zen forward), plane (mechanical info), seen (zen select), stamp (zen check), levelup (zen streak), xp (mechanical progress-step), rankup (soft level-up), secret (soft wake), rattle (zen drag-start). Files copied from design/sound-board/<set>/; js/sfx.js NORM: old files keep their pre-Zen measured gains, new cues are scaled to the old sound's peak × its gain (scratchpad apply_picks.py), so each event is as loud as before.
- 2026-10-18 · v0.1.864 · Sound levels set by measurement: scripts/sfx-levels.py measures every assets/audio/sfx file (EBU R128 max momentary loudness, padded so short clicks measure properly), compares it with a per-event target (payoffs loudest: Legendary −16, Epic/shiny/rank −17 … collect −20, arrive/stamp −22, flip/plane −24, lock −25, ticks/toggle/XP −28 LUFS, before sfx.js LEVEL 0.25), caps gains so nothing clips, and rewrites js/sfx.js NORM. Music sits ≈ −36.5 LUFS, so payoffs land ~8 dB over it, ticks just under. Rerun after swapping any sound; change a level by editing TARGET.
- 2026-10-18 · v0.1.865: secrets in the circle now ~16 px across (was 26; Sevaan: still too big). sky.js drawSecrets: scale = max(1.2, 16 / SECRET_PX[kind]).

- 2026-10-19 · v0.1.882 (Sevaan: a card swiped away seemed to vanish part-way, janky): card.js throwOff starts from the element's current transform (optional `from`, default its computed transform) instead of translate(0,0), so a card positioned or nudged by a transform no longer jumps before it flies. The catch reveal captures the holder's transform before cancelling its landing animation, and now closes first and tidies the holder 500 ms later (only if no new reveal has opened); before, cancelling the throw put the card back in the middle as the reveal faded. Checked frame by frame on the welcome card: 78 → −535 px, no jump or blank frames.

Can't be checked in the desktop harness; needs a phone.

- 2026-10-20 · Android release setup (Sevaan: "set up the Play Store"). native/android/app/build.gradle takes -PversionCode/-PversionName and signs release builds with the upload key at ~/.space-collector/upload.jks (alias upload), whose password is read from the macOS keychain (service space-collector-upload-key) at build time, never written to a file. Sevaan creates both himself (keytool + security add-generic-password, both prompt). native/build-android.sh <versionCode> makes the signed .aab (Play) and .apk (send to a friend) into ~/Desktop/Space Collector Android/. Play App Signing on, so the upload key can be reset via Play support. *.jks/*.keystore ignored. Play listing still needs Android-ratio screenshots (iPhone 1206×2622 is over Play's 2:1 limit), a 1024×500 feature graphic and a 512 icon; RevenueCat needs the goog_ key once the Play app exists. New personal Play accounts must run a closed test with 12+ opted-in testers for 14 days before production.
- 2026-10-20 · FIX (Sevaan's iPhone, 10:36 AM looking down): the sky filled with a blue web of stacked frames and a shrunken copy of itself in a brown quarter-screen. Cause: the daytime soil layer swaps sky.ctx to an offscreen canvas while drawing it; a failure there (likely an iPhone out of canvas memory: getContext returns null) left sky.ctx pointing at the hidden layer, so later frames never reached or cleared the screen. Now draw() starts every frame on sky.mainCtx with a reset state, and the soil layer is built in try/finally (restores the context; on failure the ground is plain for 10 s, soilOffUntil). Verified by forcing OffscreenCanvas.getContext to return null: the sky draws normally.

"METEOR SHOWER / Orionids tonight / <rate>. Collect any card to earn its badge. · See tonight ›"; tapping opens Up in your sky on Tonight. Not shown once the badge is earned. Tested in WebKit.

- 2026-10-20 · Android (Sevaan's friend, Chrome, phone flat on a table): the view kept jumping up to the sky and sweeping back down. Chrome stops sending orientation events when the phone is perfectly still, so after 2 s hasLiveSensors() went false, the view fell back to the drag view (looking up), and the next event blended back down. New sensors.js hasSensorView() (a basis seen in the last 10 min) now drives the view (rawBasis), the takeover blend and the target gate (liveSince); hasLiveSensors() (2 s) still drives tap-to-wake and Settings. Tested: readings stopped, view held at −90° for 8 s. Also the radar tip lost its ✕ (it sat on the text on Android); it now steps away 1.5 s after you first touch the sky, or after 30 s.
- 2026-10-20 · Bug hunt, motion and device state (agent report; fixes verified with synthetic orientation events in Chromium):
  - A tap on a still phone no longer switches to finger mode for good: pointerdown, guideTo, showMePass, aimAt, the hero/vis-map spinners and the Settings motion row use hasSensorView(); only a device that never sent a reading turns to finger mode. Verified: still 3 s, tap → still motion, view unchanged.
  - Return from background: checkMotionBack only on iPhones (needsPermission), and taps in the first 1.6 s (state.motionCheckUntil) don't drop motion. Tap to wake calls sensors.js resumeSensors(): asks permission inside the tap and only succeeds if readings arrive. The ?resume path sets needsMotionTap when motion doesn't start.
  - headingOf(basis) (back minus up·back_z) replaces atan2 of the camera axis for the radar, the turn hint, guideTo and switching to finger mode: steady pointing near the zenith (verified 348–350° under hand shake; it read 74/23/68 before). turnHint's "on screen" uses sky.project.
  - Finger mode tracks one pointer: a second finger no longer hijacks the drag (verified az held at 100.0).
  - iPhone north isn't seeded from a flat/overhead pose (|back.z| > 0.9); a reading without a compass value no longer flips the source away from 'ios'; compass doubt fades while pointing high.
  - Android absolute orientation now gets magnetic declination too (rotation vector is magnetic north; was ~10° off in Toronto). Not yet checked on a real Android against a landmark.
  - Smoothing is time-based and the idle throttle skips by time (< 30 ms), so Low Power Mode (30 fps) isn't halved to 15.
- 2026-10-20 · Bug hunt, time/network/storage/long sessions (agent report):
  - Storage (js/store.js): the open times out after 5 s and rejects when blocked (the loader could hang forever; verified it now starts); a closed/lost connection clears dbPromise (onclose, onversionchange) and tx() retries once on InvalidState/Unknown/Abort (verified: saving works after the connection is forced closed); transactions reject on abort (a commit-time failure left the promise pending and Collect dead). recordSighting's save and loadSightings have timeouts (withTimeout 8 s / 6 s).
  - Reminders: a second tap while one is being set gets the same promise (remindPending), an older schedule for the same key is cancelled first, ids never reuse one in use. Streak reminder uses fixed id 7140.
  - Streak weeks count observing nights (time − 12 h), so a session past midnight isn't two weeks; Observer stats "Best night run" uses local noon-to-noon (was UTC noon).
  - Leaks: dragScroll adds its window listeners per drag only; the horizon glow's warmth is quantised and the glow-sprite cache capped at 48 (it grew ~150 canvases a sunset).
  - Weekly recap window and label use calendar days (right across the clock change). Weather and plane fetches time out at 8 s. Sound effects retry loading if some files failed (offline first tap).
  - Checked fine by the agent: the 2026-11-01 clock change for Tonight/chip/headliner, WebGL context loss of the Milky Way, 5,000 sightings, iCloud size. Not done: the Collection iframe's own backup merge (suspected duplicate path), old web tabs mixing versions after a deploy.

Checked at phone and iPad tile sizes, before/after.

A device that gave up on motion (localStorage noMotion) goes straight to finger mode; enableMotion success clears it.

Reminders skip cloud: recheckReminders (after every forecast) takes a reminder's notification off the phone while cloud is forecast at the pass (remWatch: the pass start from the 'id@start' key, else its time) and puts it back if it clears; unknown weather never pauses; the bell stays lit. localStorage reminders now [key, id, at, title, body, paused].

- 2026-10-21 (Sevaan) page switching, after a stress test of every tab order fast and slow (13 sequences × 2 speeds, WebKit):
  - The last tab tapped wins: tab changes wait on the pill's slide through `navLater` (navSeq), and a newer tap (or the Settings button) drops a waiting one. Fixed: the Collection stuck over the sky page (fast Sky → Collection → Sky / → Explore), Settings stuck half-closed over the sky page (one shared panelHideTimer; now `panelHideTimers[id]`).
  - Settings goes back where it was opened (`settingsFrom`): from Sky, the sky page is underneath at the same scroll as it slides away (#debug.leaving z 21); from the Collection, the Collection stays open under it (panel-over-col for as long as Settings is up), no longer closed and swapped for Explore.
  - The Now hero's countdown keeps ticking while the page is hidden (it stopped as soon as it was drawn behind the closed page, so it came back as --:--).
  - The radar tip ("Tap the radar / See what's up tonight") is retired (radarTip returns at once).
  - Landscape screen (#rotate, index + cards) looks like the loader: the target circle and ticks, a phone in it turning upright as the ring locks orange, SPACE COLLECTOR, "Turn your phone upright". The apps are portrait-locked on phones, so only browsers see it; hidden inside the embedded Collection.
  - The Sun is catchable until its top edge sets (natural.js: el > −0.8, was > 2; Sevaan saw it on the horizon and a plane took the circle instead).

Design boards: design/next-card-art.html (best catch with its card, A–D) and design/sky-compass.html (big compass on tapping the radar, A–D).

Tapping the card opens it with the real picture (showTonightCard(..., { preview: true })).

Drag to look round; tap the big heading to come back; tap anything (or a row) to close and aim (passes: Show me). The sky loop pauses under panels, so the page re-propagates satellites with look() once a second. Chips: up now/new, dark until/at, Moon, sky. Lists: In front of you (±70°), Behind you. - 2026-10-21 FIX (Sevaan: Collect flashing super fast): a plane and a star/planet/Moon/Sun about equally far from the centre swapped every frame with hand tremble (Collect ↔ "Just a plane"; reproduced: 103 swaps in 3 s). findPlane now keeps whichever is showing unless the other is 0.5° nearer the centre (0 swaps after). A sweep of the whole sky and a tremble test on every visible satellite found no other flicker.
- 2026-10-21 (Sevaan: All around you showed 194 names at once): it draws only what's worth turning for (arCollect `worth`): the Moon, Sun and planets, the next 2 good passes on the horizon, and at most 8 satellites new to you, Uncommon or rarer (Commons only when fewer than 10 Uncommon+ are left; stations/icons always), rarest then brightest, one per card. Everything else is a faint unnamed dot you can't tap; deep sky is left off; only stars brighter than mag 0.3 are named. Labels that can't find room are dropped, never piled. Lists show only the worth-a-look items; the chip reads "N up · M worth a look".
- 2026-10-21 (Sevaan, slick idea 05): a heartbeat before an Epic or Legendary card turns over: the sealed card's wait is 1.7 s with two double beats (thump-thump at 0.12/0.33 s, again at 0.98/1.19 s): sfx.js heartbeat() (a low synthesized thump, follows Sounds and volume), the dim deepens and the card swells a little on each, and main buzzes medium/heavy on 'sc:heartbeat'. A tap still turns it at once; off under Reduce Motion. Sevaan also liked slick ideas 7, 8, 9, 10, 11, 13, 14, 15, 16 (design/slick-ideas.html); 6 (numbers count up) is being mocked on real cards.
- 2026-10-21 slick ideas built (Sevaan picked 5, 7, 8, 9, 10, 11, 13, 14, 15, 16 from design/slick-ideas.html; 6 and 12 declined/not picked):
  - 07 into your binder: closing a brand-new card (✕, not the flick) flies a copy of its front into the Collection tab (main.js intoBinder, revealIntoBinder set only for a first catch from the capture path), the tab bumps, "+1" pops. Not during the tour, over a page or the Collection.
  - 08 scrub the night: the Every pass bars grow in when the card first comes into view each visit (evChart, evGrown); slide a finger on the chart for a line + "11 PM · 9 up" with a tick per bar.
  - 09 compass with clicks: All around you clicks (buzz + soft toggle sound) and lights N/E/S/W as the strip's centre passes them (arFlash); a worth-a-look item sliding into the view box pings with an orange ring (arPings).
  - 10 what changed since you were here: cards-page remembers colVisit {at, xp} when the Collection is left; on the next show, XP counts up from then, the bar slides from there, "+N XP since last time" shows over the ladder, and cards first caught since wear a NEW sticker with a glint (tile-new) until opened (colPeeled; peels off).
  - 11 press and hold to peek: hold a Collection tile 380 ms → the full card lifts out over a blurred grid; let go → it drops back; the click after a peek is swallowed (holdToPeek/openPeek/closePeek).
  - 13 sheet folds into the bell: setting a pass reminder ticks the row, then the sheet shrinks into the button that opened it (lastPress → prOrigin), the bell swings with a chime and the time shows beside it (prFold). No bell on screen → the old slide-down.
  - 14 lights out: red night mode turns on with a red line sweeping down (a .night-sweep layer growing to full height, same multiply as body.night::after), off sweeps back up; one buzz (toggleNight).
  - 15 every card turns over: picking a card back (and joining Plus, not restoring) shows a fan of five of your cards that flip in a wave to the new back (cardsTurnOver; turnCard + applyBack).
  - 16 Tonight lights like a fuse: the Tonight page timeline lights row by row behind a spark, then an orange NOW line settles at the current time and creeps every 30 s (odFuse).
  All off or instant under Reduce Motion.

  - Side safe areas: the Duo's status bar is a ~84pt strip down one side (both screens). :root in css/app.css and css/collection.css defines `--safe-l` / `--safe-r` (env insets, 0 on other phones) and `--vws` = 100vw minus twice the larger side inset; every `100vw` width in app/collection/ui CSS now uses `--vws` (identical on phones). Side-anchored HUD steps in by its side's inset (header padding, radar, Next chip, meteor banner, toast tray, panels, sheets, Tonight page, recap, reveal top/news, tour deck: block at the end of css/app.css). The Collection gets the side insets from sendInsets ({sc:'insets'} left/right → --safe-l/--safe-r; main padding uses --main-pad, dialogs padded). Checked: 402×874 and 375×667 element rects identical before/after.
  - Wide windows (min-width 900px and min-height 560px; by size, not device, per Apple): the Tonight page is two columns (now/coming up/new/seen before | tonight's haul/headliner/run/shiny/missions/every pass; jump chips hidden), Settings is a 2-column grid of cards (grid-auto-rows: max-content: the scrolling panel's fixed height squeezed auto rows), Testing tools 2 columns, panels padded symmetrically by the larger inset so the 56px column gap sits on the fold; the Collection in landscape puts the rank card in a sticky 360px left column; bottom sheets max 620px, centred.

- 2026-10-22 · Duo simulator fixes (coordinator report; screenshots in the session scratchpad duo/):
  - "Collection rows collapsed" on the unfolded Duo was the page left scrolled down from the folded phone layout: the count line, your first cards and the Up tonight heading sat under the pinned tabs/search. cards-page.js scrolls to the top whenever the wide-layout media query flips (WIDE_Q). Wide landscape Collection shows 4 cards a row (`.grid[data-cols] { --cols: 4 !important }`; the right column is narrower).
  - Up tonight slot pill: two lines that never wrap, sized to its tile with container units (.up-next__tile container-type inline-size; min(12px, 12.5cqi)), so it no longer covers the card's name on small tiles.
  - Welcome card: "Swipe away" never overlaps the card: --cf (.wc-holder and .wc-stage) also caps at (100dvh − safe-top − safe-bottom − 136px) / 42, which only bites on short screens (951×669: card top 36 → 92, hint gap 5px like the SE's 6px; 669×951, 466×678, 402×874, 375×667 and iPads measured unchanged).
- 2026-10-22 · Duo picks built (Sevaan: "go with your picks", design/duo-layouts.html). They replace the two-column wide layouts above. "Book" sizes are by size, not device: on its side ≥900 × 560 landscape, upright ≥600 × 900 portrait; Duo, iPads and iPad portrait all match, phones never do. The same media query is in css/app.css, css/collection.css, main.js `bookQ` and cards-page.js `DOCK_Q`.
  - **Tonight is a book.** The left (or top) page is `#vtab-now`; the right (or bottom) page is the new `#sky-later` wrapper around the Tonight heading and `#vtab-tonight`. Each page (`[data-col]`) scrolls on its own, the 40px fold gap stays empty, and the jump chips are hidden. On phones `#sky-later` is `display: contents`, so nothing moves there.
    - Places are saved with `panelPos`/`panelRestore` (page scroll, each column, layout, section) for the Sky tab, cards opened from the page, and so on.
    - `skyJumpTo` scrolls whichever column holds the section.
    - `skyLiveSec`/`skyCol` track where you're reading. They ignore scrolls within 500 ms of a resize, because WebKit fires the reflow's scrolls before the media change. A fold or unfold mid-page lands on that section.
  - **pull-close.js:** a pull waits for the `[data-col]` page under the finger to be at its top.
  - **Settings is an index and a group.**
    - `renderSetIndex` builds `#set-index`, run on every open. Each entry shows the number, the name and its first three row names; Testing tools gets its own entry when shown.
    - `chooseSetGroup` sets `.on` on the chosen group, plus `set-about` (credits and version show with About) and `set-tools`.
    - On its side, the index is sticky in the left column (`#debug` is a grid). Upright, it's a 3 × 3 block of tabs filling the top page, so the group starts below the fold.
    - The chosen group is kept across changes. Going back to a phone scrolls to it; from a phone, the group you'd scrolled to becomes the chosen one (`setLive`).
  - **The Collection is a grid and a card.** In the book sizes the viewer opens non-modally beside the grid (`showViewerDialog`, `#viewer.docked`, `container-type: size` so the card sizes to its page). Tapping another tile or swiping swaps the card in place; the open card's tile is ringed and kept in view (`revealTile`).
    - The rank card becomes a slim bar (rank, XP, bar). With nothing open, the empty page shows `#dock-empty`.
    - Upright: with no card open the grid gets the whole screen. A card opens on the bottom page, the tabs unpin, and the ✕ sits in the corner.
    - Albums and Patches keep the whole width, and changing tab closes the docked card.
    - A docked viewer doesn't tell Explore it's modal (the switcher stays), isn't closed by `away`, and closes with Escape.
    - Fold or unfold with a card open: the card stays open, moved between docked and modal (`redocking` guards the close listeners; Up tonight's list is restored by `leaveUpList`).
  - **Header rule:** `#sky-header::after` now stops at the side insets too.
  - **Checks:** phones re-measured identical at 402×874 and 375×667 (WebKit rect diff of Explore, Tonight, Settings, Collection and the viewer; only time-dependent text differed). Fold/unfold/rotate place-keeping was tested in Chromium and WebKit. Harness: session scratchpad `duo/` (`book.mjs`, `place.mjs`, `phone.mjs`).
- 2026-10-22 · Duo simulator fixes, round 2:
  - **Flicking a card away now works everywhere.** On the capture reveal / View card, a flick that started on the card's picture began a WebKit image drag, so the pointer events stopped after one move and the card couldn't be thrown away; I reproduced this in WebKit at 669×951, 466×678 and 402×874.
    - Fix: `#reveal img` has no pointer events and can't be dragged (`dragstart` is blocked), and the flick is now read on the whole `#reveal`, not just `#rv-holder`; buttons and links keep their taps.
    - The Collection viewer's `#slot img` can't be dragged either.
  - **The reveal card is fitted by measurement.** `sizeCard` still sizes the card as before, then checks the laid-out holder against `#rv-top` above and the rank bar / View in collection below. Only if it doesn't fit does it shrink `--cf` and centre the card with an inline `--rv-y`, so phones are unchanged (checked at 951×669, 669×951, 466×678, two iPad sizes, 402×874, 375×667 and 320×568).
  - **Zoom is snapped back.** `unzoom()` resets a page left zoomed in (`visualViewport.scale` > 1, from a pinch or a double-tap) by briefly adding `maximum-scale=1` to the viewport meta. A zoomed page is the likeliest cause of the 433 pt card with its top bar off screen, which I couldn't reproduce on desktop. Technical now shows `zoom` beside the screen size.
  - **Larger Text is capped at 115% on big screens.** When the shorter side is 560+ (unfolded Duo, iPads) the scale stops at 115%; phones still go to 135%. It's re-checked on resize (fold/unfold).
  - **The docked card page ends above Explore's tabs** (`bottom: safe-bottom + 96px`). Card sizes were refitted to the shorter page.

- 2026-10-22 · Widgets built (iOS, not yet on a device). Pieces:
  - js/main.js `shareWidgets`/`sendWidgets`: builds tonight's plan for the widgets (`chain` = the next catch from each moment on, using the Now hero's rule; `list`, `run`, haul, 16 busyness bars, weather, missions left, plus) and calls the native `Widgets` plugin. Sent after tonight's plan, each forecast, each catch, Plus changing and when the app is hidden; not while time-travelling. `openWidgetLink` handles spacecollector://pass/<id>@<start> (Show me), …/tonight and …/plus, including a cold start (App.getLaunchUrl); held until the sky is up.
  - native/ios/App/App/WidgetBridge.swift (`Widgets` plugin, registered in MainViewController): writes plan.json to the App Group group.<app id> and copies each named small card picture as art/<name>.png (240 px), then reloads all timelines.
  - native/ios/App/SCWidgets (extension, iOS 17+): one widget "SpaceCollectorNext" for systemSmall/Medium/Large and accessoryCircular/Rectangular/Inline; style A (picture in the target circle, ring = ProgressView(timerInterval:) emptying over the last hour); moments: day (dark in), next, up now, cloudy, late night, no plan. Fonts Russo One + Barlow Condensed (UIAppFonts, files from fonts/). Without Plus every family shows the Plus face and opens the Plus sheet.
  - Project: added with the xcodeproj gem (scratchpad add_widgets.rb). New build setting SC_APP_ID (project level): the app's PRODUCT_BUNDLE_IDENTIFIER = $(SC_APP_ID), the widget's = $(SC_APP_ID).widgets, App Groups entitlement group.$(SC_APP_ID) on both; build-tester.sh now passes SC_APP_ID=…tester (never override PRODUCT_BUNDLE_IDENTIFIER, it would give the widget the app's id). URL scheme `spacecollector` in Info.plist. CURRENT_PROJECT_VERSION lives in both targets now: bump all four configs.
  - Simulator: build with CODE_SIGN_IDENTITY=- CODE_SIGN_STYLE=Manual DEVELOPMENT_TEAM= (ad hoc, so the App Group works); checked small, medium, large on an iPhone 18 Pro simulator with real data. Device/TestFlight builds will need App Groups registered for both app ids (automatic signing with -allowProvisioningUpdates should do it).
  - Plus card perk "Widgets · Tonight on your home and lock screen." under Rare alerts.

- 2026-10-22 (Sevaan: "Do it", design/compass-calibrate.html): **Line up the sky** replaces the ±5° compass nudge. Settings → Aiming: Compass · Line up (#calib-open), Last lined up (#calib-last "Against the Moon · 2 h ago", #nudge-label the total), Reset (#nudge-reset, clears calibInfo too). Line up (main.js openCalib) closes the page, hides the HUD (body.calib) and shows one card (#calib): `calibRef()` picks the Moon (10–70° up, lit), else by dark the brightest planet, else the brightest star under mag 1.5, never the Sun. Steps (calibStep): motion (Line up first tries to turn motion on inside the tap; the card offers it again if it failed), eight (figure-8 when an iPhone compass reports doubt), aim (A: point the circle at it, "It's in the circle" → `calibAimAt(az)` nudges by az − headingOf(trueBasis), lockHeading), drag (B: sideways drag on #sky turns it, "Turned 7° east" pill, Set), north (no reference: point north, or East/South/West). Cancel restores the starting nudge; "Reset to the phone's compass" clears it. Saved as the existing compassNudge plus localStorage calibInfo {at, ref, deg}; a "Sky lined up · Turned 95° west, against the Moon" notice. Phones that only give relative orientation get a "Sky drifting? · Line it up" standing message that opens it. Tested with synthetic orientation (Chromium + WebKit): aim twice → second pass "already right"; north consistent; drag shows the turn live; Cancel restores exactly.
- 2026-10-22 (Sevaan: "if it's daytime there is no moon"): by day Line up uses your shadow. calibRef: the Moon first (if up), else with the Sun 5°+ up a `shadow` reference at the Sun's azimuth + 180° ("Use your shadow · Point the circle along your shadow · Stand with the Sun behind you and aim at the top of your shadow's head. Never look at the Sun." → "Along my shadow"); else (twilight) north; at night planets/stars. Tested at 1 PM: turned 12°, then "already right".

Now: assets/audio/sfx/lock.mp3 softened (8 ms fade-in + 4.5 kHz low-pass; original in the session scratchpad lock-before.mp3) and its target −53 LUFS (sfx-levels.py, NORM lock 0.35). Played peak −21.7 → −34.5 dB (about 25 dB under a typical sound's peak), loudest 50 ms −40.5 → −50.0 dB. For "half as loud" use −10 dB, not −6. The homepage's own copy (site/public/home/audio/lock.mp3) is unchanged.

- 2026-10-22 bug hunt, code review batch:
  - orbit.js loadCatalog deleted `el` from every object, which switched off in the app (not standalone cards.html) last chance cards (fallAt), their tier bump, the Witness patch, the falling bonus in the headliner score, and the orbit globe on card art. `el` now stays (16,710 objects; 5+ falling today, e.g. Swift, Aureole 2).
  - setReminderNow: a reminder that didn't get set (prompt declined, schedule error) is no longer saved, so its bell doesn't light after a relaunch.
  - weeklyRecap fallback named the oldest sighting of the week, not the last (sightings are newest first).
  - Collection "Up tonight" slots skip passes that have set (the plan is only re-read every ~20 min).
  - sfx()/heartbeat() do nothing while the page is hidden (a ticket fired in the background woke the suspended audio context).
  - plus.js initStore registers its appStateChange listener once (each failed configure retry added another).
  - progress() takes `lon` (progressNow passes state.observer.lon), so tonight's missions are read under the same night key they were dealt with (they split when you travelled).
  - onBackupChange refreshes fossilDone/ufoCaught/secretFound, so an iCloud restore doesn't re-earn them.
  - Left alone: xpCarry2/rankFloor2 latching 0 if the log hadn't loaded (only affects pre-2026-10-10 players; recomputing risks giving new players free XP).
- 2026-10-22 bug hunt, performance batch (baseline numbers and scripts: scratchpad perf/ of that session; summary here):
  - Workers (tonight, week-ahead nights, pass, find) get `toWorker(list)` (no satrec) and rebuild satrecs with `withSatrecs` (orbit.js). Cloning 16,710 objects: 103 ms → 26 ms on a Mac (~4x less on a phone). Tonight plan checked identical (80 passes) before/after. The find-worker test stubs withSatrecs.
  - Google Fonts (Inter, Plex Mono) load with media=print/onload, so first paint doesn't wait on Google (offline, weak signal). Both pages.
  - toast.js fitStack skips its layout read when no ticket is in or the page is hidden (it ran 3.3x/s forever).
  - Tried and reverted: queueing Collection tile builds a few per frame. Big-album scroll jank in headless WebKit was the same over already-built tiles (paint, not building), so it didn't help there; check on a real phone before redoing.
  - Not done (bigger changes, ask first): split fleets out of catalog.json, share parsed lore/facts with the Collection iframe, lazy stinger sounds, 2048px Milky Way texture, modulepreload, shorter native loader floor (1.4 s is Sevaan's 2026-10-08 call).

- 2026-10-22 (Sevaan's answers to the bug-hunt questions):
  - Cards follow Settings → Units: js/units.js useMiles() (same rule as main.js setUnits, readable from the Collection page); card.js inUnits converts km/km-h stats to mi/mph at render, keeping about the km number's figures (Moon 384,400 km → 238,900 mi); orbit-view's "Now over…" line too. Changing Units refreshes the Collection (not at start-up: colFrame isn't defined yet, which froze the loader once). share-card reads a stat value as all of <b>'s text except <small> (the decimal point is an <i>).

  - Settings location note in plain words: "Using where you are now", the searched place's name, "Your last location" (no coordinates).
